Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the devices framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 365. The resulting operator achieves bound O(91/n^4).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the devices space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 365 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 91. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any devices module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 906. The projective resolution has length at most 227 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on devices structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 906. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 227. QED.
